## Standing lessons (promoted from session entries — survive all collapses)

Cross-cutting rules that have crystallized across the arc. These are the institutional memory the collapse cadence preserves; each is stated once here and not re-told per session entry.

- **Emit the ENTIRE CURRENT_STATE.md at every session close, never a delta/patch.** A prior chat emitted only a delta; it was missed and the file fell a session behind (caught + regenerated 2026-06-01).
- **Keep git current at session close.** The hand-promote workflow + uncommitted git (anchored at M13 while na3b/na3c/na3d lived only as working files) is what turned the NA-3d promote into a recovery. Commit the canonical at close.
- **Derive calendars programmatically** from windows + declared pauses (precedence: pause > plant > harvest > growing > wait) — never hand-place month arrays. Hand-placement caused a calendar↔`heat_pause`-object coherence bug the gate caught (NA-3c) and is the placement-error class eliminated by deriving.
- **Verify per source; don't assume the inherited framing.** "Mirror se_gulf" was wrong for fl_peninsula once VH021 was read (single-window, not two-window). The kickoff said "verify"; verification overturned the assumption. Status does NOT inherit — Step 5 side-by-side confers it.
- **Window structure is decided PER SOURCE** (se_gulf two-window vs. fl_peninsula single-window); **re-derive vs. lift is a source-guard call** — lift only from clean region-appropriate zone calendars, re-derive (no `lifted_from_zone`) where the legacy zone band is contaminated.
- **PENDING/marker guards target LIVE DATA SLOTS, not provenance prose.** A blanket "PENDING substring" check tripped on the legitimate `plantings_provenance.supersedes: "PENDING scaffold…"` line (NA-3d). The cell was correct; the guard was over-strict.
- **Lifted-and-assumed-done is suspect until re-verified.** `northern_tier` was lifted verbatim in Phase A and treated as done; it carried real gaps. NA-3h reconciled it (succession migration + two winter-calendar defects that a remap-not-derive shortcut had preserved — caught only because Step 5 mandates deriving calendars from windows, and because gates (a) ">=1 cold_pause" + (b) "active ⊆ windows" both ran; monotonicity alone would have missed the zero-pause z7). A verbatim lift carries forward old gaps AND misses everything the standard added since. **The lesson generalizes: any "wrapped, not worked" cell needs the full bar, and a remap of stale data is NOT a derivation.**
- **Arc POSITION is a derived value, not a carried claim — re-derive it from the checklist, never from the prior kickoff's prose.** (2026-06-01) A next-chat kickoff stated "all 10 region cells done → Step 11 flag-flip next," conflating "the region-cell SLICE is done" with "the ARC is done." The region work is Steps 4 + 5 + per-cell 5.5 — ONE slice; the arc also owes Step 6 (depth-lift), Steps 7-8 (siblings), and the Step 11 whole-crop validation pass. This is the SAME failure class as the reverted 2026-05-29 M13 flip (trust an assertion instead of re-deriving from the source of truth). It reached only PROSE (CURRENT_STATE + a kickoff), never a flipped flag, because the Step 11 gate re-derives readiness from the crop, not from a kickoff. **Rule: every kickoff opens by reading the gold-standard-arc-checklist and deriving the next unowned step from the live crop state + the checklist; if the kickoff's prose and the checklist conflict, the checklist wins. Kickoffs SUMMARIZE; they are not authority on arc position.** (Tooling caught nothing here; Trevor did, after three pushes — the human-readable narration layer is the weak point, the mechanical Step 11 gate is the real defense.)
- **A corrected header on a stale body is WORSE than a uniformly-stale file** — it contradicts itself and a reader can't tell which half to trust. (2026-06-01) Banner-only patching after NA-3h left the body three sessions behind (still "8 of 10, NA-3f next"). **This is exactly why the rule is "emit the ENTIRE file every close": the whole file moves forward together, header AND body AND SHA lines AND next-pointers AND gate-status.** Patching one section reintroduces the drift.
- **Stale-overwrite policy:** pre-v1.4 / pre-this-arc artifacts are STALE and overwritten as correct findings surface (exercised on se_gulf's §6 calendar drift, NA-3d — narrowly, finding-scoped, not a blanket rewrite).
- **Two-clean-results is a sample of 2, not proof the rest are clean.** Accuracy over velocity; surface scope choices to Trevor, don't pivot mid-session.
- **README paste hygiene:** session-close command blocks are commands-only, no inline `#` comments or `(...)` (zsh chokes); explanation goes in prose above. **BUNDLE-PATH GUARD:** derive promotion `mv`/`cp` paths from actual `unzip -l` output, not the assumed working dir.
- **Two-field readiness predicate, never a bare count:** `blocks_launch AND status != "resolved"` (6 resolved findings retain `blocks_launch:true`).
- **Step 10 anchoring-URL hygiene must be scoped BY LAYER, and anchoring backfill is Step-11 evidence work, not a Step-10 rewrite.** (2026-06-04) A naive whole-crop walk for `sources`/`anchoring_urls` pairing flagged 80 "missing" -- but most are the established two-layer shape: legacy `zones{}` carries `sources` with empty `anchoring_urls:{}` (the deferred re-sourcing layer, finding_001 family), and `regions{}` anchors per-arm at `plantings[].direct_sow/harvest_*` + `resolved_by_zone.N.heat_pause` (region-root + container levels legitimately carry none). Only crop-field-level and genuinely-empty per-arm leaves are real gaps. **And closing those gaps is fetch+verify (Step 4/5 evidence + the schema `launch_ready_core` gate), NOT voice rewriting** -- Step 10 (the IP/voice lane) DETECTS + REGISTERS them, Step 11 closes them. Checklist amendment (7) owed to encode this.
- **The schema `launch_ready_core` gate is an INDEPENDENT readiness check from the two-field findings predicate.** (2026-06-04) Anchoring-URL completeness (one entry per source ID on every all-mode field) is required by the schema gate (Surface 4) even when `blocks_launch AND status != "resolved"` returns 0. A crop can show 0 unresolved blockers AND still fail the flip on anchoring gaps. Step 11 must run BOTH.

---

- **A registered gap’s described REMEDIATION PATH is itself an unverified claim until checked against the live data.** (2026-06-04) S10-3 catalogued the `northern_tier` anchoring gap with the note “the cold legacy `zones[3–7]` hold the trustworthy URLs to promote up”; this rode forward verbatim into CURRENT_STATE line 63 and the kickoff. Verified at backfill time: the legacy `zones[3–7]` ALSO carry empty `anchoring_urls:{}` — there was nothing to promote. The NA-3h “trustworthy copy” judgment was correct for PLANTING DATA (promoted + re-verified, `zone_promoted_verified`) but was silently extended to URLs, which the legacy layer never had (it predates per-source anchoring). Impact was citation-only, not data (no date moved). **The lesson generalizes the “lifted-and-assumed-done” + “verify per source” rules one step further: it is not only LIFTED DATA that needs re-verification, but the GAP-REMEDIATION INSTRUCTIONS a prior session writes about how a future gap will be closed. “The URLs exist to promote” is a claim, not a fact, until the source slot is opened. Confirm the remediation path empirically before scoping work around it — and when a precedent says “promote-up where the legacy band is trustworthy,” scope it to CONTENT, never URLs (else the bot pipeline replicates the empty-URL assumption to ~114 crops).** Caught at backfill preflight, surfaced to Trevor; CURRENT_STATE line 63 corrected same session.

- **DISPLAY-READINESS is a SEPARATE BAR from CERT, and a downstream surface can expose it (2026-06-15, session `lemon_spacing_backfill`).** lemon was certified (`verified_gs_arc`, `launch_ready` x2) but shipped empty `spacing_inches`; the plant-astro garden calculator gates crop PLACEABILITY on a non-empty `spacing_inches`, so a certified (and hero-arted) lemon was invisible in the tool while orange-navel -- which happened to carry spacing -- appeared. Root cause: the GS-arc definition-of-done validated biology + sources but never required `spacing_inches`/`companions`, the fields the consumer tool actually reads. **Fix:** a SOURCED back-fill (`spacing_inches:[180,300]` = UF/IFAS HS1153 "15 to 25 feet" + a `spacing_inches_anchoring_urls` entry; surgical/additive, only lemon, cert intact, whole_crop_gate PASS), with the lesson locked into CURRENT_STATE guardrails: back-fill any certified crop missing `spacing_inches`, and ADD `spacing_inches`(+`companions`) to the GS-arc DoD so future certs ship display-ready. **Generalizes the verification-status-vs-display-readiness distinction:** a `launch_ready` flag certifies the DATA's biology, NOT that every field a downstream surface needs is populated -- audit display-readiness separately, per consuming surface. Spacing is a SOURCED figure (never guess it); per-rootstock spread/height left null because HS1153 gives only a generic maintained size, not per-rootstock figures.

*Update this file at each session close.*
